<generate_description_from_diff>
<description># Summary

This pull request implements several significant improvements to the Meeting2Jira application:

1. **Enhanced Jira User Handling**: Improved error handling when creating tickets with invalid assignees
2. **Frontend UI Overhaul**: Complete redesign using Tailwind CSS with a Jira-inspired color scheme
3. **Improved UX**: Added toast notifications, better visual feedback, and streamlined configuration flow
4. **Route Guards**: Added navigation guards to ensure proper setup flow
5. **Dependency Updates**: Added Tailwind CSS, PostCSS, and Autoprefixer to the frontend

# Files Changed

## Backend

📄 `backend/src/services/jira.service.ts`

Improved error handling for Jira user assignment. When a specified assignee cannot be found in Jira, the service now logs a warning and creates the ticket without an assignee rather than failing completely. This prevents ticket creation failures due to invalid usernames.

## Frontend - Configuration & Dependencies

📄 `frontend/package.json`

Added Tailwind CSS dependencies (tailwindcss, autoprefixer, postcss) to enable the new UI styling system.

📄 `frontend/package-lock.json`

Lockfile updates reflecting the new Tailwind CSS dependencies and their transitive dependencies.

📄 `frontend/tailwind.config.js`

New Tailwind configuration file defining custom color palette inspired by Jira's design system, including colors for navigation, cards, borders, status indicators, and text.

## Frontend - Core Application

📄 `frontend/src/app/app.component.ts`

Refactored root component to remove Angular Material dependencies and add toast notification support. Now displays connection status indicator and settings gear icon in the navigation bar.

📄 `frontend/src/app/app.component.html`

Redesigned navigation bar with Jira-inspired styling using Tailwind classes. Added connection status indicator and settings button.

📄 `frontend/src/app/app.component.css`

Removed all custom CSS as styling is now handled by Tailwind utility classes in the template.

📄 `frontend/src/app/app.routes.ts`

Updated routing configuration to redirect to setup page by default and added route guards to enforce proper configuration flow before accessing main features.

## Frontend - Route Guards

📄 `frontend/src/app/guards/config.guard.ts`

New route guard implementation to protect routes that require Jira configuration. Redirects users to setup page if not configured, and prevents access to setup page if already configured.

## Frontend - Services

📄 `frontend/src/app/services/toast.service.ts`

New toast notification service providing success, error, warning, and info notifications with auto-dismiss functionality and optional action links.

## Frontend - Components

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/upload/upload.component.ts`

Enhanced upload component with inline Jira configuration editing, improved file size formatting, processing step visualization, and toast notifications replacing snackbars.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/upload/upload.component.html`

Complete redesign with two-column layout showing Jira connection status alongside upload interface. Added animated processing steps and improved visual feedback during extraction.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/upload/upload.component.css`

Removed custom CSS in favor of Tailwind utility classes.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/settings/settings.component.ts`

Refactored settings component to work as initial setup screen with improved flow. Added "Continue to App" functionality for first-time setup and better error messaging.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/settings/settings.component.html`

Redesigned as a centered card-based setup form with Jira branding, clearer field labels, and improved validation feedback.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/settings/settings.component.css`

Removed custom CSS in favor of Tailwind utility classes.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/review/review.component.ts`

Refactored to remove Angular Material dependencies and add toast notifications. Improved ticket creation flow with individual success notifications including links to created tickets.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/review/review.component.html`

Complete redesign with Jira-inspired ticket cards, sticky toolbar, confidence badges, and improved visual hierarchy. Added low-confidence warnings and success banners for created tickets.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/review/review.component.css`

Removed most custom CSS, keeping only essential transition effects while relying on Tailwind for layout and styling.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/toast/toast.component.ts`

New toast notification component providing slide-in animations, type-specific icons and colors, and optional action links. Supports auto-dismiss and manual dismissal.

## Frontend - Global Styles

📄 `frontend/src/styles.css`

Replaced Angular Material theme import with Tailwind directives. Added custom scrollbar styling for dark theme consistency.</description>
</generate_description_from_diff>

<title>Frontend Redesign: Jira-Inspired UI with Enhanced UX and Bug Fixes</title>
<body># Summary

This pull request implements several significant improvements to the Meeting2Jira application:

1. **Enhanced Jira User Handling**: Improved error handling when creating tickets with invalid assignees
2. **Frontend UI Overhaul**: Complete redesign using Tailwind CSS with a Jira-inspired color scheme
3. **Improved UX**: Added toast notifications, better visual feedback, and streamlined configuration flow
4. **Route Guards**: Added navigation guards to ensure proper setup flow
5. **Dependency Updates**: Added Tailwind CSS, PostCSS, and Autoprefixer to the frontend

# Files Changed

## Backend

📄 `backend/src/services/jira.service.ts`

Improved error handling for Jira user assignment. When a specified assignee cannot be found in Jira, the service now logs a warning and creates the ticket without an assignee rather than failing completely. This prevents ticket creation failures due to invalid usernames.

## Frontend - Configuration & Dependencies

📄 `frontend/package.json`

Added Tailwind CSS dependencies (tailwindcss, autoprefixer, postcss) to enable the new UI styling system.

📄 `frontend/package-lock.json`

Lockfile updates reflecting the new Tailwind CSS dependencies and their transitive dependencies.

📄 `frontend/tailwind.config.js`

New Tailwind configuration file defining custom color palette inspired by Jira's design system, including colors for navigation, cards, borders, status indicators, and text.

## Frontend - Core Application

📄 `frontend/src/app/app.component.ts`

Refactored root component to remove Angular Material dependencies and add toast notification support. Now displays connection status indicator and settings gear icon in the navigation bar.

📄 `frontend/src/app/app.component.html`

Redesigned navigation bar with Jira-inspired styling using Tailwind classes. Added connection status indicator and settings button.

📄 `frontend/src/app/app.component.css`

Removed all custom CSS as styling is now handled by Tailwind utility classes in the template.

📄 `frontend/src/app/app.routes.ts`

Updated routing configuration to redirect to setup page by default and added route guards to enforce proper configuration flow before accessing main features.

## Frontend - Route Guards

📄 `frontend/src/app/guards/config.guard.ts`

New route guard implementation to protect routes that require Jira configuration. Redirects users to setup page if not configured, and prevents access to setup page if already configured.

## Frontend - Services

📄 `frontend/src/app/services/toast.service.ts`

New toast notification service providing success, error, warning, and info notifications with auto-dismiss functionality and optional action links.

## Frontend - Components

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/upload/upload.component.ts`

Enhanced upload component with inline Jira configuration editing, improved file size formatting, processing step visualization, and toast notifications replacing snackbars.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/upload/upload.component.html`

Complete redesign with two-column layout showing Jira connection status alongside upload interface. Added animated processing steps and improved visual feedback during extraction.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/upload/upload.component.css`

Removed custom CSS in favor of Tailwind utility classes.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/settings/settings.component.ts`

Refactored settings component to work as initial setup screen with improved flow. Added "Continue to App" functionality for first-time setup and better error messaging.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/settings/settings.component.html`

Redesigned as a centered card-based setup form with Jira branding, clearer field labels, and improved validation feedback.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/settings/settings.component.css`

Removed custom CSS in favor of Tailwind utility classes.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/review/review.component.ts`

Refactored to remove Angular Material dependencies and add toast notifications. Improved ticket creation flow with individual success notifications including links to created tickets.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/review/review.component.html`

Complete redesign with Jira-inspired ticket cards, sticky toolbar, confidence badges, and improved visual hierarchy. Added low-confidence warnings and success banners for created tickets.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/review/review.component.css`

Removed most custom CSS, keeping only essential transition effects while relying on Tailwind for layout and styling.

📄 `frontend/src/app/components/toast/toast.component.ts`

New toast notification component providing slide-in animations, type-specific icons and colors, and optional action links. Supports auto-dismiss and manual dismissal.

## Frontend - Global Styles

📄 `frontend/src/styles.css`

Replaced Angular Material theme import with Tailwind directives. Added custom scrollbar styling for dark theme consistency.</body>
